Garrison 0.7.3 — buildings and unit model audit

Checked editable source, compiled private records, inherited official attachments, private model/stamp selections, age tiers and equipment. All 1,267 verified package member hashes were rechecked. This audit changed no gameplay, art, normal-edition source or delivery.

Concurrent source drift: two Hideout files separately changed build time 60 to 30 seconds during the audit. Exact byte comparisons confirm no rendering fields changed. See concurrent-source-drift.json. The package comparison remains against verified 0.7.3.

Custom geometry = authored/remodeled meshes. Material remix = stock geometry with changed materials. Partial = custom additions with a stock main component. Stock = no selected private replacement. An asset existing in the archive does not establish that a consumer selects it.

Important finding

The active Water Buffalo selects the private material-remixed Sultanate armored elephant model. It does not select a dedicated buffalo-shaped model. This is a static binding finding; native appearance was not tested.

Components and limits

  • Rattan research has no qualified activated woven-armor/back-shield visual. Retained authored experiments do not count as active custom assets.
  • Six older equipment material selections exist, but only five are selected by the inspected local actor/equipment graph: recurve bow, crossbow, arbalest, basic spear, monk staff III. The ji-spear remix is retained without a selected edge.
  • Ram m016, Springald m017 and ji-spear m023 are retained model selections without a selected edge in the inspected default local actor/equipment graph. Fixing such bindings may avoid creating replacement assets from scratch.
  • Horse anatomy, elephant anatomy, stock work tools/cargo, many weapons, siege mechanisms and unresolved human siege crews remain native or material-only. A custom unit body does not mean every component is bespoke.
  • A missing custom design is not a missing game resource: stock fallback assets can work normally. This report does not authorize gameplay/art changes.
  • Static production/construction edges are candidates; requirements, civilization branches and SCAR conversions can change actual availability. Sparse campaign/expansion records may inherit default visual fields.
  • Source and package graphs do not prove in-game appearance, animation, lifecycle behavior, save/load or multiplayer.

Verification

376 tuning instances: 0 errors, 130 pre-existing warnings. Current official inheritance: 198 local / 5,758 official blueprints, 0 errors. .NET XmlReader parsed 381 source files. All 305 historical unit payload hashes match the current package, confirming the geometry-versus-material provenance for 57 retained model selections. The audit includes 102 local non-projectile/non-weapon actors, 77 private squads, and 267 inspected official records.
